#### Synthesis Summary
14 material issue(s) require attention. Highest-priority issue: issue-001 (high) — Post-release correction handling is materially under-modeled: a corrected LIS result can leave Report.result_status appearing finalized and clinician-trusted until delayed propagation, while the ontology also lacks versioned correction/amendment evidence to reconstruct what changed. Controlled deliberation did not leave a blocking unresolved disagreement for the highest-priority issue.

#### Classification Summary
- Highest severity: high
- Severity counts: blocker=0, high=5, medium=9, low=1, info=0
- Finding count: 19
- Root-cause issue count: 15
- Material issue count: 14
- Non-material finding count: 1

#### Material Issues
- issue-001 (high)
  - affected purpose: Use the ontology as the EMR/LIS integration concept authority for result/report states, clinician-facing status trust, and operational-risk review. Source finding context: Use as the conceptual authority document for EMR/LIS integration and operational-risk review. Source finding context: Use the ontology as the authoritative model for result/report states in EMR/LIS integration.
  - failure condition: A final result is corrected after report release and systems need to display, exchange, audit, or trust the report status before or during amendment propagation. Source finding context: A Result.status changes to corrected after report release, while Report.result_status remains finalized until the nightly batch. Source finding context: A final result is corrected after release and systems need to exchange, audit, or display the amendment history, but only current status fields exist.
  - impact: Clinician-facing report status can appear authoritative while lagging the LIS correction state, and downstream systems cannot reconstruct the amendment event or preserve the meaning of previously released reports. Source finding context: The authority document tells integrators and clinicians that Report.result_status is trusted, yet permits a bounded stale state that can misrepresent corrected results during clinical use. Source finding context: The model cannot reconstruct post-final changes or preserve the clinical meaning of prior released reports, reducing trust in report-state authority.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-001`, `materialized-input.md:69-72`, `materialized-input.md:80-83`, `materialized-input.md:121-123`, `review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11`, `materialized-input.md:69-72, 75-84, 121-123; review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-003`, `materialized-input.md:62-84`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:62-84,121-123`
  - source lenses: axiology, coverage
  - action candidates: fix_now
  - problem definition: Post-release correction and amendment semantics are missing, so clinician-facing report status can remain trusted while stale and amendment history cannot be reconstructed.
  - problem framing: root_cause / current_blocker / fix_now / must_close_in_target / inferred

- issue-002 (high)
  - affected purpose: Conceptual authority for EMR/LIS integration with emphasis on operational risks.
  - failure condition: A critical value is marked notified=true while the integration model has no authoritative notification time, recipient, acknowledgement, or reconciliation path to the external phone log.
  - impact: Integrators cannot derive a reliable operational contract for immediate notification, audit, or cross-system consistency from the authority document.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-002`, `materialized-input.md:100-106`, `review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11`, `materialized-input.md:100-106; review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11`
  - source lenses: axiology
  - action candidates: fix_now
  - problem definition: Critical-value notification authority is reduced to a boolean while the evidence needed for immediate-risk handling remains outside the ontology.
  - problem framing: root_cause / current_blocker / fix_now / must_close_in_target / inferred

- issue-005 (high)
  - affected purpose: Use the ontology as a concept authority for EMR/LIS integration and operational risk around lab reporting.
  - failure condition: A critical result is generated and notification status must be exchanged, audited, reconciled, or escalated across systems, but the ontology exposes only a boolean and externalizes the evidence.
  - impact: Patient-safety-critical communication becomes under-specified; systems may treat a flag as sufficient without shared evidence for recipient, timing, responsibility, or acknowledgement.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-002`, `materialized-input.md:100-106`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:100-106`
  - source lenses: coverage
  - action candidates: fix_now
  - problem definition: Critical-value notification lacks an in-ontology event or evidence concept for actor, time, recipient, method, acknowledgement, and basis.
  - problem framing: root_cause / current_blocker / fix_now / must_close_in_target / inferred

- issue-007 (high)
  - affected purpose: Provide a concept authority for EMR/LIS integration from test ordering through assay execution and result reporting. Source finding context: EMR/LIS 연동 설계의 개념 권위 문서로서 검사 주문 카탈로그와 LIS 수행 단위의 안정적 연결을 제공하는 목적. Source finding context: 검사 주문부터 분석 수행 및 결과 보고까지의 EMR/LIS 개념 권위 제공 목적. Source finding context: EMR/LIS 통합의 개념 권위 문서로서 주문 카탈로그와 LIS 수행 단위를 연결하는 구조적 완결성
  - failure condition: New tests, analyzer-specific assays, panel changes, or result mappings require deciding whether Test and Assay are the same item, separate units, or linked codes. Source finding context: 새 검사 항목, 장비별 assay, 패널 구성 변경을 추가할 때 Test와 Assay를 각각 수정해야 하지만 두 개념 사이의 권위 매핑이 없다. Source finding context: 신규 검사 항목이나 결과 매핑을 구현할 때 Test와 Assay가 같은 항목인지, 다른 수행 단위인지, 어떤 코드가 결과를 권위 있게 식별하는지 결정해야 할 때 실패한다. Source finding context: LIS가 Assay 단위로 수행/결과를 만들거나 신규 항목을 Test와 Assay 양쪽에 등록하는 운영 상황
  - impact: Implementers can diverge on order-code to assay-code mapping, omit double registration, or lose traceability between ordered tests, actual performed assays, and produced results. Source finding context: 통합 구현자가 주문 코드와 수행 코드를 독립 카탈로그로 해석하거나 중복 등록 누락을 만들 수 있어, 확장 시 기존 주문/결과 데이터의 추적성과 호환성이 약해진다. Source finding context: 카탈로그 중복, 주문-수행-결과 매핑 오류, 분석기 코드와 EMR 주문 코드의 의미 드리프트가 생길 수 있어 통합 설계의 핵심 개념 신뢰가 약해진다. Source finding context: 주문된 Test와 실제 수행 Assay/Result 사이의 권위 경로가 없어 인터페이스 매핑, 결과 추적, 카탈로그 정비 기준이 문서 안에서 닫히지 않습니다.

- issue-009 (high)
  - affected purpose: Use of the ontology as the concept authority for EMR/LIS integration state behavior.
  - failure condition: A Result changes from final to corrected after the Order has transitioned to completed under the all-Result-final rule. Source finding context: A Result changes from `final` to `corrected` after the Order has transitioned to `completed` under the 'all Result final' rule.
  - impact: Systems consuming the ontology can derive incompatible closure states for the same order, weakening trust in state synchronization and closure semantics. Source finding context: Systems consuming the ontology can derive incompatible closure states for the same order: the Order remains completed while its defining completion condition is no longer true, weakening trust in state synchronization and closure semantics.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/logic.findings.yaml#logic-candidate-001`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:12`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:100`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:101`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:12, clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:100, clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:101`
  - source lenses: logic
  - action candidates: fix_now
  - problem definition: Order completion is defined over mutable Result.status values without declaring whether completion is a snapshot, live invariant, or reversible lifecycle state.
  - problem framing: root_cause / current_blocker / fix_now / must_close_in_target / inferred

- issue-004 (medium)
  - affected purpose: Use the ontology as the EMR/LIS integration concept authority for clinical lab workflow entities, relations, and states.
  - failure condition: After a specimen reaches analyzed, downstream systems need to exchange or reason about storage, retention, disposal, or later audit/rework, but the ontology has no concept for those states. Source finding context: After a specimen reaches `analyzed`, downstream systems need to exchange or reason about storage, retention, disposal, or later audit/rework, but the ontology has no concept for those states.
  - impact: The authority document cannot close the specimen lifecycle it explicitly references, weakening integration scope control and leaving operationally important states to undocumented departmental interpretations.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-001`, `materialized-input.md:37-44`, `materialized-input.md:121-124`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:37-44,121-124`
  - source lenses: coverage
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - problem definition: The Specimen lifecycle stops at analyzed even though post-analysis handling such as retention, storage, transfer, and disposal is in scope by reference.
  - problem framing: root_cause / next_step_blocker / carry_forward / must_close_before_next_stage / inferred

  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-003`, `state_rules[0]`, `relations: Order->Specimen, Specimen->Result, Order->Report, Report->Result`, `materialized-input.md: state_rules[0] says Order completes when all Result are final; relations provide Order->Specimen->Result and Order->Report->Result paths but no direct Order-Result membership relation`
  - source lenses: structure
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - problem definition: Order completion lacks an authoritative Result membership set or snapshot boundary despite multiple indirect Order-to-Result paths.
  - problem framing: root_cause / next_step_blocker / carry_forward / must_close_before_next_stage / inferred

#### Synthesized Material Issue Explanations
- issue-001 (high): Post-release correction handling is materially under-modeled: a corrected LIS result can leave Report.result_status appearing finalized and clinician-trusted until delayed propagation, while the ontology also lacks versioned correction/amendment evidence to reconstruct what changed.
  - issue stance agreement: 6/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: axiology, coverage, evolution, logic, structure, semantics
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
  - not applicable lenses: none
  - insufficient evidence lenses: none
  - resolution accepted by: 2/2 deliberation participants
  - accepted lenses: axiology, coverage
  - remaining disagreement: 0/2 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: none
  - raised by lenses: axiology, coverage
  - issue statement: Post-release correction handling is under-modeled: Report.result_status can remain clinician-trusted while stale, and the model cannot reconstruct correction/amendment history. Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml / Report.result_status synchronization rule Source finding context: materialized-input.md:69-72, 75-84, 121-123; review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11 Source finding context: Nightly synchronization of corrected results undermines the ontology's stated clinician-trust purpose for Report.result_status. Source finding context: The review purpose is to judge whether this file is suitable as the EMR/LIS integration concept authority document, especially operational risks. Within that purpose, an authoritative status that clinicians are expected to trust should not knowingly lag behind the LIS result correction state without an explicit safety boundary, warning state, or precedence rule. The current model accepts a hidden tradeoff: batch simplicity over timely clinical status authority. Source finding context: Make the correction propagation contract explicit and safety-preserving: either require synchronous/near-real-time Report.result_status amendment when Result.status becomes corrected, or introduce an explicit pending-correction/stale-report state with clinician-facing visibility, precedence rules, and maximum propagation latency. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-001 Source finding context: Use as the conceptual authority document for EMR/LIS integration and operational-risk review. Source finding context: A Result.status changes to corrected after report release, while Report.result_status remains finalized until the nightly batch. Source finding context: The authority document tells integrators and clinicians that Report.result_status is trusted, yet permits a bounded stale state that can misrepresent corrected results during clinical use. Source finding context: The ontology assigns clinician-trusted authority to Report.result_status while delegating correction propagation to a delayed batch without modeling stale/pending authority states. Source finding context: Report.result_status is described as the authoritative displayed value trusted by clinicians. Source finding context: Corrected LIS Result.status changes are propagated to Report.result_status only by a once-nightly synchronization batch. Source finding context: That delayed propagation weakens the review intent of using the ontology as an EMR/LIS integration authority for operational risk. Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml / Result and Report correction lifecycle Source finding context: .onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:62-84,121-123 Source finding context: Final-result correction and report amendment are represented as statuses but not as versioned correction events. Source finding context: Correcting a finalized lab result changes a clinically consumed record. A coverage-complete lifecycle must support post-final correction without losing the previous released fact or the reason and authority for the amendment. The current model covers the status label but not the operational evidence needed to reconstruct what changed and when. Source finding context: Introduce ResultCorrection and ReportAmendment/version concepts, including previous value/status, new value/status, reason, corrected_by, corrected_at, amended_report_version, released_at/released_by, and links back to the affected Result and Report. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-003 Source finding context: Use the ontology as the authoritative model for result/report states in EMR/LIS integration. Source finding context: A final result is corrected after release and systems need to exchange, audit, or display the amendment history, but only current status fields exist. Source finding context: The model cannot reconstruct post-final changes or preserve the clinical meaning of prior released reports, reducing trust in report-state authority. Source finding context: The ontology models correction/amendment as terminal-looking status values without separate lifecycle event/version concepts. Source finding context: Result status includes `corrected` and Report result_status includes `amended`. Source finding context: The state rule states that when Result.status changes to corrected, Report.result_status should become amended. Source finding context: No correction event, amendment version, prior value, reason, actor, or correction timestamp is defined.
  - affected purpose: Use the ontology as the EMR/LIS integration concept authority for result/report states, clinician-facing status trust, and operational-risk review. Source finding context: Use as the conceptual authority document for EMR/LIS integration and operational-risk review. Source finding context: Use the ontology as the authoritative model for result/report states in EMR/LIS integration.
  - failure condition: A final result is corrected after report release and systems need to display, exchange, audit, or trust the report status before or during amendment propagation. Source finding context: A Result.status changes to corrected after report release, while Report.result_status remains finalized until the nightly batch. Source finding context: A final result is corrected after release and systems need to exchange, audit, or display the amendment history, but only current status fields exist.
  - impact: Clinician-facing report status can appear authoritative while lagging the LIS correction state, and downstream systems cannot reconstruct the amendment event or preserve the meaning of previously released reports. Source finding context: The authority document tells integrators and clinicians that Report.result_status is trusted, yet permits a bounded stale state that can misrepresent corrected results during clinical use. Source finding context: The model cannot reconstruct post-final changes or preserve the clinical meaning of prior released reports, reducing trust in report-state authority.
  - root hypothesis: The ontology treats corrected results and amended reports as mutable status labels with delayed propagation, without a safety-preserving amendment lifecycle, stale-state boundary, or versioned correction evidence.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-001`, `materialized-input.md:69-72`, `materialized-input.md:80-83`, `materialized-input.md:121-123`, `review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11`, `materialized-input.md:69-72, 75-84, 121-123; review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-003`, `materialized-input.md:62-84`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:62-84,121-123`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-001`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-006`, `rel-008`, `finding-relation-graph.yaml#rel-008`, `finding-001.cause-001`, `finding-001.cause-002`, `finding-001.cause-003`, `finding-006.cause-001`, `finding-006.cause-002`, `finding-006.cause-003`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-001.axiology`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-001.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-001.evolution`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-003`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:64`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:75`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:117`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-001.logic`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/issue-ledger.yaml#issue-001`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/finding-relation-graph.yaml#rel-007`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-001.semantics`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-001`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-001.structure`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-003`, `rel-006`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-004`, `rel-007`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: axiology, coverage
  - action candidates: fix_now
  - materiality: This weakens the declared purpose because the ontology is meant to be the EMR/LIS integration authority for result/report state and clinician-facing trust. If Report.result_status is authoritative but can knowingly lag behind Result.status after a correction, downstream systems may display or exchange a stale trusted status during clinical use, and audit consumers cannot preserve the meaning of earlier released reports.
  - root cause: The starting point is that correction and amendment are modeled as mutable status labels with delayed synchronization, not as a safety-preserving lifecycle. Because there is no explicit stale or pending boundary, propagation timing rule, amendment version, prior value, reason, actor, or timestamp, both the trust problem and the missing history problem follow from the same modeling gap.
  - causal path: The causal path combines two accepted findings. First, Report.result_status is treated as the clinician-trusted displayed authority, while corrected Result.status changes are propagated only by a nightly batch; this creates a period where the displayed report state can remain finalized after the underlying result has changed. Second, the model includes corrected/amended status values and a corrected-to-amended rule, but defines no correction event or report amendment version, so systems cannot reconstruct the post-final change or its authority.
  - action: Define an explicit correction/amendment lifecycle now. The repair should specify synchronous or near-real-time amendment behavior, or an explicit clinician-visible pending/stale state with precedence and maximum latency, and add versioned ResultCorrection and ReportAmendment evidence including previous/new values or statuses, reason, actor, timestamps, report version, release metadata, and links to the affected Result and Report.

- issue-002 (high): CriticalValue notification authority is materially insufficient: the ontology records only notified=true/false while the operational evidence needed for immediate critical-value handling remains outside the model.
  - issue stance agreement: 6/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: axiology, coverage, structure, evolution, logic, semantics
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
  - not applicable lenses: none
  - insufficient evidence lenses: none
  - resolution accepted by: 1/1 deliberation participants
  - accepted lenses: axiology
  - remaining disagreement: 0/1 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: none
  - raised by lenses: axiology
  - issue statement: CriticalValue notification authority is too thin for immediate-risk handling because notification time, recipient, acknowledgement, and reconciliation are outside the ontology. Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml / CriticalValue notification authority Source finding context: materialized-input.md:100-106; review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11 Source finding context: CriticalValue models only a notification boolean while placing notification time and recipient outside the ontology, weakening its operational authority for immediate-risk handling. Source finding context: For an EMR/LIS integration concept authority document, immediate critical-value notification is a purpose-critical operational workflow. A boolean alone cannot represent who was notified, when, through what channel, or how EMR/LIS should reconcile the external phone log. The model therefore accepts an unexamined tradeoff that leaves a safety-critical commitment outside the conceptual authority surface. Source finding context: Promote notification evidence into the ontology: add a CriticalValueNotification or NotificationEvent entity with notified_at, recipient, notifier, channel, acknowledgement/status, and relations to CriticalValue/Result/Order. If the phone log remains authoritative, model it as an external authority with reconciliation and precedence rules. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-002 Source finding context: Conceptual authority for EMR/LIS integration with emphasis on operational risks. Source finding context: A critical value is marked notified=true while the integration model has no authoritative notification time, recipient, acknowledgement, or reconciliation path to the external phone log. Source finding context: Integrators cannot derive a reliable operational contract for immediate notification, audit, or cross-system consistency from the authority document. Source finding context: The ontology treats critical-value notification evidence as external to the model while retaining only a lossy boolean inside the authority document. Source finding context: CriticalValue is explicitly an immediate-notification risk concept. Source finding context: The ontology records only notified:boolean and states the actual notification time and recipient are in a separate telephone log. Source finding context: This prevents the file from fully serving as EMR/LIS integration concept authority for that operational-risk workflow.
  - affected purpose: Conceptual authority for EMR/LIS integration with emphasis on operational risks.
  - failure condition: A critical value is marked notified=true while the integration model has no authoritative notification time, recipient, acknowledgement, or reconciliation path to the external phone log.
  - impact: Integrators cannot derive a reliable operational contract for immediate notification, audit, or cross-system consistency from the authority document.
  - root hypothesis: The ontology keeps only a lossy CriticalValue notification boolean while notification evidence remains outside the authority model.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-002`, `materialized-input.md:100-106`, `review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11`, `materialized-input.md:100-106; review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-002`, `finding-002.cause-001`, `materialized-input.md:100-101`, `finding-002.cause-002`, `materialized-input.md:102-106`, `finding-002.cause-003`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-002.axiology`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-002.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-002.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-002.logic`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/issue-ledger.yaml#issue-002`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-002.semantics`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-003`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-002.structure`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: axiology
  - action candidates: fix_now
  - materiality: This weakens the stated purpose because the ontology is meant to serve as conceptual authority for EMR/LIS integration with operational-risk emphasis. For critical values, integrators need to know who was notified, when, by whom, through which channel, whether acknowledgement occurred, and how any external phone log reconciles with EMR/LIS state. A boolean cannot support immediate notification, audit, or cross-system consistency as an authoritative contract.
  - root cause: The root cause starts at the ontology boundary: notification evidence is externalized while only a lossy CriticalValue notification boolean remains inside the authority model. Because the model does not define a notification event or external authority relationship, downstream systems cannot infer what notified=true actually certifies.
  - causal path: The causal path is: CriticalValue is explicitly an immediate-notification risk concept; the ontology then represents notification only as notified:boolean and places actual notification time and recipient in a separate telephone log; therefore the ontology cannot fully serve as EMR/LIS integration authority for this safety workflow. Supporting lenses accepted the high severity, with some narrowing the concern to event semantics, boolean truth conditions, or change resilience rather than disputing the issue.
  - action: Promote critical-value notification evidence into the ontology by adding a CriticalValueNotification or NotificationEvent with notified_at, recipient, notifier, channel, acknowledgement/status, and relations to CriticalValue, Result, and Order. If the telephone log remains authoritative, model it explicitly as an external authority with reconciliation and precedence rules before relying on notified=true as an integration contract.

- issue-005 (high): Critical-value notification is materially under-modeled: the ontology treats notification as a boolean while the safety-critical communication event and its proof are outside ontology authority.
  - issue stance agreement: 6/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: axiology, coverage, semantics, structure, evolution, logic
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
  - not applicable lenses: none
  - insufficient evidence lenses: none

  - accepted lenses: coverage
  - remaining disagreement: 0/1 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: none
  - raised by lenses: coverage
  - issue statement: Critical-value notification lacks an in-ontology evidence concept for actor, time, recipient, method, acknowledgement, and basis. Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml / CriticalValue notification model Source finding context: .onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:100-106 Source finding context: Critical value notification lacks an in-ontology evidence concept for actor, time, recipient, and basis. Source finding context: Critical value notification is a controlled safety action, not just a flag. Coverage guidance asks whether actions needing control have actor/time/basis evidence concepts. By keeping evidence outside the ontology, EMR/LIS integrations can disagree on whether notification was performed, who received it, and what proof supports it. Source finding context: Add a critical-value notification event/entity linked to CriticalValue/Result/Order/Staff, with notified_at, notified_by, recipient, recipient_role/contact, method, acknowledgement status/time, escalation path, and evidence/source reference. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-002 Source finding context: Use the ontology as a concept authority for EMR/LIS integration and operational risk around lab reporting. Source finding context: A critical result is generated and notification status must be exchanged, audited, reconciled, or escalated across systems, but the ontology exposes only a boolean and externalizes the evidence. Source finding context: Patient-safety-critical communication becomes under-specified; systems may treat a flag as sufficient without shared evidence for recipient, timing, responsibility, or acknowledgement. Source finding context: The CriticalValue model represents notification as a boolean and places notification evidence in an external phone log not modeled by the ontology. Source finding context: CriticalValue defines `notified` as a boolean. Source finding context: The note says notification time and recipient are in a phone record ledger, not in the ontology. Source finding context: No in-ontology notification event or acknowledgement evidence concept exists for actor, time, recipient, method, or basis.
  - affected purpose: Use the ontology as a concept authority for EMR/LIS integration and operational risk around lab reporting.
  - failure condition: A critical result is generated and notification status must be exchanged, audited, reconciled, or escalated across systems, but the ontology exposes only a boolean and externalizes the evidence.
  - impact: Patient-safety-critical communication becomes under-specified; systems may treat a flag as sufficient without shared evidence for recipient, timing, responsibility, or acknowledgement.
  - root hypothesis: The CriticalValue model represents notification as a boolean and places operational proof in an external phone log not modeled by the ontology.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-002`, `materialized-input.md:100-106`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:100-106`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-005`, `finding-005.cause-001`, `finding-005.cause-002`, `finding-005.cause-003`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-005.axiology`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-005.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-005.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-005.logic`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/issue-ledger.yaml#issue-005`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/finding-relation-graph.yaml#rel-003`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-005.semantics`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-003`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-005.structure`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-002`, `rel-003`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: coverage
  - action candidates: fix_now
  - materiality: This weakens the stated purpose because EMR/LIS integration and operational risk management require shared authority over whether a critical result was actually communicated, to whom, when, by whom, by what method, with what acknowledgement, and on what evidentiary basis. A boolean cannot support reliable exchange, audit, reconciliation, or escalation for patient-safety-critical reporting.
  - root cause: The starting point is the CriticalValue model's choice to represent notification as `notified` while placing operational proof in an external phone log. Because the ontology does not own the notification event or evidence concept, downstream systems lack a common source of truth for the facts that make `notified=true` meaningful.
  - causal path: The causal chain is: CriticalValue defines notification as a boolean; the note externalizes notification time and recipient to a phone record ledger; therefore the ontology has no event or acknowledgement evidence concept for actor, time, recipient, method, basis, or proof. That leaves integrations free to disagree on whether notification happened and whether acknowledgement or escalation obligations were satisfied.
  - action: Add a critical-value notification event or entity linked to CriticalValue, Result, Order, and Staff, with fields for notified_at, notified_by, recipient, recipient role/contact, method, acknowledgement status/time, escalation path, and evidence/source reference. This must be fixed in the target authority model so notification evidence becomes exchangeable, auditable, and reconcilable rather than remaining an external side record.

- issue-007 (high): Test와 Assay가 별도 카탈로그로 유지되지만 이를 닫는 canonical mapping이나 실행 관계가 없어, 주문 가능한 검사(Test), 실제 수행 assay, 결과(Result)를 권위 있게 연결할 수 없습니다.

  - issue statement: The Test-Assay catalog split is not closed by a canonical mapping or execution relation, so orderable tests, performed assays, and results cannot be reliably connected. Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml entities.Test / entities.Assay catalog model Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:41, clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:49, clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:121 Source finding context: 신규 검사 항목 추가 시 Test와 Assay를 이중 등록해야 해 카탈로그 확장 내성이 낮다. Source finding context: EMR/LIS 통합의 개념 권위 문서라면 주문 카탈로그와 수행 카탈로그 사이의 확장 경로가 안정적이어야 한다. 현재 구조에서는 새 검사, 패널 분해, 장비별 assay 변경이 생길 때 두 카탈로그를 동시에 수정해야 하며 어느 쪽이 권위인지 판단할 수 없어 기존 주문/결과 연결의 연속성이 깨질 수 있다. Source finding context: Test와 Assay를 즉시 통합하지 않더라도 canonical catalog item 또는 explicit mapping relation을 추가하고, 신규 항목 등록의 단일 권위와 버전/유효기간을 정의한다. 예: Test has_many AssayMapping, Assay performs Test 또는 CatalogItem을 부모 개념으로 두고 주문/수행 projection을 분리한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-001 Source finding context: EMR/LIS 연동 설계의 개념 권위 문서로서 검사 주문 카탈로그와 LIS 수행 단위의 안정적 연결을 제공하는 목적. Source finding context: 새 검사 항목, 장비별 assay, 패널 구성 변경을 추가할 때 Test와 Assay를 각각 수정해야 하지만 두 개념 사이의 권위 매핑이 없다. Source finding context: 통합 구현자가 주문 코드와 수행 코드를 독립 카탈로그로 해석하거나 중복 등록 누락을 만들 수 있어, 확장 시 기존 주문/결과 데이터의 추적성과 호환성이 약해진다. Source finding context: 주문 카탈로그(Test)와 수행 카탈로그(Assay)의 분리 여부는 명시했지만 두 개념 사이의 canonical mapping/authority concept를 모델에 두지 않았다. Source finding context: Test는 주문 가능한 카탈로그 단위로 정의되어 있다. Source finding context: Assay는 분석기에서 수행되는 검사 카탈로그의 수행 단위로 별도 정의되어 있다. Source finding context: 현재 신규 항목은 두 곳 모두에 등록한다고 note가 명시한다. Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml: Test and Assay catalog concepts Source finding context: .onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:46, .onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:54, .onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:127 Source finding context: Test와 Assay의 의미 경계가 중첩되지만 둘 사이의 권위 관계가 없다. Source finding context: 주문 단위와 분석 수행 단위는 실제 업무에서 다를 수 있지만, 이 문서에서는 둘 다 검사 항목/카탈로그 단위로 이름 붙어 있고 중복 등록까지 요구한다. EMR/LIS 통합의 개념 권위 문서라면 주문 코드, 수행 코드, 결과 코드의 의미 경계와 매핑 권위가 핵심인데, 현재 모델은 동의어인지 계층인지 crosswalk인지 판단할 수 없다. Source finding context: Test를 orderable test, Assay를 performed assay로 명확히 분리하고 Test-Assay 매핑 관계(예: ordered_as/performed_by, one-to-many 가능성)를 추가한다. 신규 항목 등록 권위도 한 카탈로그와 매핑 절차로 정리하거나, 두 카탈로그를 유지한다면 동기화/식별자 의미를 명시한다. Source finding context: 카탈로그 중복, 주문-수행-결과 매핑 오류, 분석기 코드와 EMR 주문 코드의 의미 드리프트가 생길 수 있어 통합 설계의 핵심 개념 신뢰가 약해진다. Source finding context: 주문 가능한 검사와 분석기 수행 항목을 별도 엔티티로 두었지만 의미 경계와 매핑 관계를 정의하지 않았다. Source finding context: Test와 Assay가 모두 검사 항목/카탈로그 단위로 정의되어 의미가 겹친다. Source finding context: 신규 항목은 두 곳 모두에 등록한다고 되어 있어 두 카탈로그가 병행 권위처럼 작동한다. Source finding context: Assay가 Test/Specimen/Result 실행 경로에 연결되지 않은 상태입니다. Source finding context: 문서가 EMR/LIS 통합의 개념 권위 문서라면 주문 카탈로그(Test)와 LIS/분석기 수행 단위(Assay) 사이의 연결이 핵심 경로입니다. 현재 구조에서는 신규 항목을 Test와 Assay 양쪽에 등록한다고 하면서도 둘을 매핑하는 관계가 없어, 어떤 주문 Test가 어떤 Assay 수행과 Result 생성으로 이어지는지 권위 있게 추적할 수 없습니다.   - impact: Implementers can diverge on order-code to assay-code mapping, omit double registration, or lose traceability between ordered tests, actual performed assays, and produced results.   - source lenses: evolution, semantics, structure
  - action candidates: fix_now
  - materiality: 이 온톨로지의 목적은 검사 주문부터 분석 수행과 결과 보고까지 EMR/LIS 통합의 개념 권위를 제공하는 것입니다. 현재 구조에서는 신규 검사, 장비별 assay, 패널 변경, 결과 매핑이 생길 때 주문 코드와 수행 코드의 관계를 문서 내부에서 결정할 수 없어 구현자별 해석, 중복 등록 누락, 추적성 손실이 발생할 수 있습니다.
  - root cause: 문제의 출발점은 Test와 Assay를 각각 주문 카탈로그와 수행 카탈로그처럼 정의하면서도, 어느 쪽이 권위인지와 둘 사이의 매핑, 버전, 유효기간, 실행 경로를 모델에 두지 않은 것입니다. 이 결여 때문에 Assay가 핵심 order-to-result 경로에 참여하지 못하고 독립된 카탈로그처럼 남습니다.
  - causal path: Test는 주문 가능한 단위이고 Assay는 분석기 수행 단위로 정의되어 있으며, 신규 항목은 두 곳 모두에 등록한다고 되어 있습니다. 그러나 relations에는 Assay를 포함하는 연결이 없어 Test와 Assay의 이중 등록이 같은 항목인지, 계층인지, crosswalk인지 검증할 수 없습니다. Result도 Test만 참조하므로 실제 수행 Assay가 어떤 결과를 만들었는지까지 이어지는 권위 경로가 닫히지 않습니다.
  - action: Test-Assay 사이에 canonical mapping 또는 상위 CatalogItem 개념을 추가하고, 단일 등록 권위와 버전/유효기간 의미를 정의해야 합니다. 또한 orderable Test에서 performed Assay를 거쳐 Result로 이어지는 명시적 실행 관계를 추가해, 신규 항목과 장비별 변경이 기존 주문/결과 추적성을 깨지 않도록 해야 합니다.

- issue-009 (high): Order.completed is unstable as currently defined: an Order can remain completed after a Result moves from final to corrected, even though the all-results-final condition that justified completion is no longer true.
  - issue stance agreement: 6/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: axiology, coverage, evolution, logic, semantics, structure
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
  - not applicable lenses: none
  - insufficient evidence lenses: none
  - resolution accepted by: 1/1 deliberation participants
  - accepted lenses: logic
  - remaining disagreement: 0/1 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: none
  - raised by lenses: logic
  - issue statement: Order.completed can remain true after the Result.status condition that caused completion is no longer true. Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml state_rules / Order lifecycle Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:12, clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:100, clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:101 Source finding context: Order completion is defined by all Result values being final, while the same model permits later correction of Result.status after report finalization. Source finding context: The completion predicate depends on a reversible state (`Result.status=final`). Because the model also allows a subsequent `corrected` state, a workflow path can satisfy `Order.completed` at time T and later invalidate the condition that caused completion at time T+1. Within the declared rules, the model cannot keep both 'completed because all results are final' and 'a result is now corrected' logically stable unless completion is explicitly snapshot-based or an Order can leave/revise `completed`. This matters for an EMR/LIS authority document because downstream systems may treat Order completion as closed while Report/Result state continues to change. Source finding context: Define whether `Order.completed` is a snapshot release milestone or a live invariant. If it is live, add reopening/amended-order transitions for corrected results. If it is a milestone, rewrite the rule to say completion is based on all reportable results being final at release time and is not invalidated by later corrections, with separate amendment tracking. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/logic.findings.yaml#logic-candidate-001 Source finding context: Use of the ontology as the concept authority for EMR/LIS integration state behavior. Source finding context: A Result changes from `final` to `corrected` after the Order has transitioned to `completed` under the 'all Result final' rule. Source finding context: Systems consuming the ontology can derive incompatible closure states for the same order: the Order remains completed while its defining completion condition is no longer true, weakening trust in state synchronization and closure semantics. Source finding context: The completion rule uses a mutable Result status as an invariant-like condition without specifying whether completion is snapshot-based or reversible. Source finding context: `Order` includes `completed` in its lifecycle. Source finding context: The ontology states that an Order transitions to completed when all Results are final. Source finding context: The ontology also states that a Result.status can later change to corrected and then requires Report.result_status to become amended. Source finding context: Because no Order reopening or amendment transition is defined, a later corrected result can invalidate the condition used to justify completed while the Order lifecycle still remains completed.
  - affected purpose: Use of the ontology as the concept authority for EMR/LIS integration state behavior.
  - failure condition: A Result changes from final to corrected after the Order has transitioned to completed under the all-Result-final rule. Source finding context: A Result changes from `final` to `corrected` after the Order has transitioned to `completed` under the 'all Result final' rule.
  - impact: Systems consuming the ontology can derive incompatible closure states for the same order, weakening trust in state synchronization and closure semantics. Source finding context: Systems consuming the ontology can derive incompatible closure states for the same order: the Order remains completed while its defining completion condition is no longer true, weakening trust in state synchronization and closure semantics.
  - root hypothesis: Order completion is defined over mutable Result.status values without specifying whether completion is a snapshot milestone, a live invariant, or a reversible/amendable lifecycle state.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/logic.findings.yaml#logic-candidate-001`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:12`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:100`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:101`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:12, clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:100, clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:101`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-011`, `finding-011.cause-001`, `finding-011.cause-002`, `finding-011.cause-003`, `finding-011.cause-004`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-009.axiology`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-009.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-009.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-009.logic`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/issue-ledger.yaml#issue-009`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-009.semantics`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-001`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-009.structure`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-004`, `rel-007`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-006`, `rel-015`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: logic
  - action candidates: fix_now
  - materiality: This weakens the ontology's purpose as an EMR/LIS integration authority because downstream systems may treat Order.completed as a closed synchronization state while Result and Report state continues to change. Consumers can therefore derive incompatible closure meanings for the same order, reducing trust in lifecycle and state synchronization semantics.
  - root cause: The causal chain starts with Order completion being defined over mutable Result.status values without declaring whether completion is a release-time snapshot, a live invariant, or a reversible/amendable lifecycle state. That missing time-boundary decision makes the completion predicate behave like an invariant while the model still permits later status correction.
  - causal path: The ontology includes completed in the Order lifecycle, then says an Order transitions to completed when all Results are final. It also permits a Result.status to later become corrected and requires the Report.result_status to become amended. Because no Order reopening, amendment, or snapshot semantics are defined, the later correction can invalidate the condition that caused completion while Order.completed remains true.
  - action: Define Order completion explicitly as either a release-time snapshot milestone or a live invariant. If it is a snapshot milestone, rewrite the rule so completion is based on all reportable results being final at release time and add separate amendment tracking for later corrections. If it is live, add reopening or amended-order transitions so corrected results can revise the Order lifecycle consistently.

- issue-003 (medium): Turnaround-time(TAT) is named as an operational metric, but its executable calculation authority remains in a downstream dashboard formula. This makes the ontology weaker as the shared EMR/LIS authority because consumers can report different TAT values while still appearing to use the same workflow model.
  - issue stance agreement: 6/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: axiology, coverage, evolution, logic, semantics, structure
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
  - not applicable lenses: none
  - insufficient evidence lenses: none
  - resolution accepted by: 1/1 deliberation participants
  - accepted lenses: axiology
  - remaining disagreement: 0/1 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: none
  - raised by lenses: axiology
  - issue statement: Turnaround-time calculation authority is split from the ontology, creating metric drift risk for EMR/LIS operational reporting. Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml / TAT ownership note Source finding context: materialized-input.md:126-128; review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11 Source finding context: Delegating turnaround-time calculation to a dashboard-owned formula creates purpose drift away from a shared EMR/LIS concept authority. Source finding context: A concept authority document for EMR/LIS integration should make shared operational metrics traceable to canonical source concepts. Allowing a downstream dashboard team to maintain its own calculation formula without an explicit derivation contract creates a hidden tradeoff: local reporting flexibility over cross-system metric consistency. Source finding context: Define TAT as a derived concept in the ontology with source fields, inclusions/exclusions, null/late-release handling, and allowed projections. If dashboards may override it, model those overrides as non-authoritative views with clear naming and precedence. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-003 Source finding context: Shared conceptual authority for EMR/LIS integration and operational-risk review. Source finding context: Different consumers compute TAT from local formulas while the ontology remains the supposed authority for the workflow concepts. Source finding context: Operational reporting can diverge from the canonical workflow model, reducing trust in the ontology as the integration source of truth. Source finding context: The ontology declares a metric boundary but leaves the executable calculation authority with a downstream dashboard team. Source finding context: The notes define TAT from collected_at to released_at. Source finding context: The same note says the dashboard team maintains its own calculation formula. Source finding context: That split weakens the document's role as the EMR/LIS integration concept authority for operational metrics.
  - affected purpose: Shared conceptual authority for EMR/LIS integration and operational-risk review.
  - failure condition: Different consumers compute TAT from local formulas while the ontology remains the supposed authority for the workflow concepts.
  - impact: Operational reporting can diverge from the canonical workflow model, reducing trust in the ontology as the integration source of truth.
  - root hypothesis: The ontology declares a TAT metric boundary but leaves executable calculation authority with a downstream dashboard formula.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-003`, `materialized-input.md:126-128`, `review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11`, `materialized-input.md:126-128; review-value-alignment-criteria.yaml:6-11`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-003`, `finding-003.cause-001`, `finding-003.cause-002`, `finding-003.cause-003`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-003.axiology`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-003.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-003.evolution`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-002`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-003.logic`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/issue-ledger.yaml#issue-003`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-003.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-003.structure`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: axiology
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - materiality: The affected purpose is shared conceptual authority for EMR/LIS integration and operational-risk review. When TAT is not canonically defined as a derived ontology concept, operational reporting can diverge across EMR, LIS, and dashboard consumers, reducing trust in the ontology as the source of truth for shared workflow metrics.
  - root cause: The causal chain starts where the ontology declares the TAT metric boundary but does not own the executable derivation contract. Because the dashboard team maintains its own formula, the ontology names the metric without controlling the source fields, calculation rule, versioning, exclusions, null handling, or precedence needed to keep the metric reproducible.
  - causal path: The evidence path is that TAT is described as running from collected_at to released_at, but the same note leaves the actual formula with a dashboard team. That split allows local formulas to become the operative definition, so different consumers can compute different TAT values while the ontology still claims to define the workflow concept. The lenses broadly support this issue; structure narrows the framing to the calculation authority boundary rather than proving an observed reporting mismatch.
  - action: Define TAT as a derived ontology concept before the next stage, including authoritative source fields, inclusion and exclusion rules, null and late-release handling, formula ownership, version or effective-period semantics, and allowed projections. If dashboards need local variants, model them as explicitly named non-authoritative views or overrides with clear precedence so reporting flexibility does not replace the canonical metric.
  - unresolved disagreement: No material disagreement remains; the only narrowing is that the issue is about missing calculation authority and precedence, not a proven numeric dashboard mismatch within the available boundary.

- issue-004 (medium): Specimen lifecycle is materially incomplete because the ontology ends Specimen state at analyzed while acknowledging post-analysis handling outside the authority model.
  - issue stance agreement: 5/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: axiology, coverage, evolution, semantics, structure
  - issue stance disagreement: 1/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: logic
  - not applicable lenses: none

  - remaining disagreement: 0/1 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: none
  - raised by lenses: coverage
  - issue statement: Specimen lifecycle omits post-analysis retention, storage, transfer, and disposal states despite referencing external post-analysis handling. Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml / Specimen lifecycle Source finding context: .onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:37-44,121-124 Source finding context: Specimen lifecycle omits post-analysis retention and disposal states. Source finding context: For a document intended as the EMR/LIS integration concept authority, specimen state must remain interpretable after analysis when reports are amended, audits occur, or recollection/discard decisions are needed. The target itself acknowledges post-analysis handling exists but leaves it outside the ontology, so the lifecycle is incomplete from coverage perspective. Source finding context: Extend Specimen with explicit post-analysis states such as `stored`, `retained`, `disposed`, `transferred`, or `rejected/discarded` as appropriate, and add attributes/events for disposition time, responsible actor, retention policy, and reason. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-001 Source finding context: Use the ontology as the EMR/LIS integration concept authority for clinical lab workflow entities, relations, and states. Source finding context: After a specimen reaches `analyzed`, downstream systems need to exchange or reason about storage, retention, disposal, or later audit/rework, but the ontology has no concept for those states. Source finding context: The authority document cannot close the specimen lifecycle it explicitly references, weakening integration scope control and leaving operationally important states to undocumented departmental interpretations. Source finding context: The ontology stops Specimen lifecycle at `analyzed` and delegates post-analysis handling to external departmental policy instead of modeling it. Source finding context: Specimen lifecycle is declared as `[collected, received, in_analysis, analyzed]`. Source finding context: The state rules state that handling after `analyzed` follows each department's internal policy. Source finding context: Because no post-analysis states or disposition evidence concepts are present, retention/disposal cannot be represented in the authority ontology.
  - affected purpose: Use the ontology as the EMR/LIS integration concept authority for clinical lab workflow entities, relations, and states.
  - failure condition: After a specimen reaches analyzed, downstream systems need to exchange or reason about storage, retention, disposal, or later audit/rework, but the ontology has no concept for those states. Source finding context: After a specimen reaches `analyzed`, downstream systems need to exchange or reason about storage, retention, disposal, or later audit/rework, but the ontology has no concept for those states.
  - impact: The authority document cannot close the specimen lifecycle it explicitly references, weakening integration scope control and leaving operationally important states to undocumented departmental interpretations.
  - root hypothesis: The ontology stops the Specimen lifecycle at analyzed and delegates post-analysis handling to departmental policy instead of modeling disposition states.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-001`, `materialized-input.md:37-44`, `materialized-input.md:121-124`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:37-44,121-124`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-004`, `finding-004.cause-001`, `finding-004.cause-002`, `finding-004.cause-003`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-004.axiology`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-004.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-004.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-004.logic`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/issue-ledger.yaml#issue-004`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-004.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-004.structure`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: coverage
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - materiality: The declared purpose is to serve as the EMR/LIS integration concept authority for clinical lab workflow entities, relations, and states. When specimens need to be stored, retained, transferred, disposed, audited, amended, or reconsidered after analysis, downstream systems have no authoritative states or disposition evidence to exchange or interpret. That leaves operationally important lifecycle meaning to departmental policy instead of the shared ontology.
  - root cause: The starting point is that the Specimen lifecycle is modeled only through collected, received, in_analysis, and analyzed, then post-analysis handling is delegated to departmental policy. Because the ontology makes analyzed the terminal modeled state, no later disposition states or evidence concepts can be derived from the current lifecycle.
  - causal path: The causal path is direct: the lifecycle declaration stops at analyzed; the state rules say handling after analyzed follows each department's internal policy; therefore retention, storage, transfer, disposal, and related disposition evidence are absent from the authority ontology. This supports the issue because post-analysis specimen status cannot be represented consistently when reports are amended, audits occur, or discard/recollection decisions are needed.
  - action: Extend Specimen with explicit post-analysis disposition states such as stored, retained, transferred, disposed, and rejected or discarded as appropriate. Add disposition evidence fields or events for disposition time, responsible actor, retention policy, and reason, so lifecycle closure is modeled before the ontology is used as the integration authority.

Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml / Test, Assay, and turnaround-time authority Source finding context: .onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:46-60,126-128 Source finding context: Parallel catalog and metric definitions lack single authority and effective-history concepts. Source finding context: Coverage guidance calls out missing single authority for values managed across multiple systems or ledgers, and missing history for time-dependent values. Catalog mappings and operational metrics are exactly the kind of integration concepts that drift when there is no authority or validity period. As an EMR/LIS concept authority, the ontology should make those boundaries explicit rather than leave them as later cleanup.   - root hypothesis: Shared, time-dependent integration values are embedded as local fields instead of governed code or authority concepts with mappings, versions, and effective history.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-004`, `materialized-input.md:46-60`, `materialized-input.md:126-128`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:46-60,126-128`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-002`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:37`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:47`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:55`, `clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:37, clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:47, clinical-lab-ontology.yaml:55`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-007`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-009`, `rel-014`, `finding-relation-graph.yaml#rel-014`, `finding-007.cause-001`, `finding-007.cause-002`, `materialized-input.md:126-127`, `finding-007.cause-003`, `materialized-input.md:128`, `finding-007.cause-004`, `finding-009.cause-001`, `finding-009.cause-002`, `finding-009.cause-003`, `finding-009.cause-004`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-006.axiology`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-006.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-006.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-006.logic`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/issue-ledger.yaml#issue-006`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/finding-relation-graph.yaml#rel-014`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-006.semantics`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-002`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-006.structure`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-005`, `rel-013`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: coverage, evolution
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - materiality: This weakens the declared purpose of controlling EMR/LIS integration scope, precedence, and change resilience. When new specimen types, test/assay catalog changes, or TAT formula changes appear, multiple systems must reconcile the same operational meaning, but the ontology leaves that reconciliation to local fields, free strings, parallel registration, or dashboard-maintained formulas. That makes compatibility rules drift outside the ontology and makes historical reconstruction unreliable.
  - root cause: The root cause starts at the modeling choice to embed shared, time-dependent integration values inside local entity properties rather than promote them into governed concepts. Because specimen kind, catalog identity, test-assay mapping, metric formula authority, owner, version, status, and effective period are not first-class concepts, later systems have no stable authority for equality, precedence, deprecation, or time-bounded interpretation.
  - causal path: The causal path is supported by two compatible findings. The coverage finding shows that Test and Assay overlap as separate catalog/performance concepts, new items are registered in both while consolidation is deferred, and TAT is calculated under dashboard ownership without modeled formula authority or history. The evolution finding shows that Specimen.specimen_type and Test.requires_specimen_type use closed enums while Assay.specimen_kind uses freer, more detailed strings, so specimen evolution requires both schema changes and informal mapping conventions. Together these paths show scattered local values becoming conflicting integration truth when shared values change over time.
  - action: Promote the shared values into compact governed concepts before the next modeling stage: define reusable specimen/code concepts with labels, aliases, broad category, LIS codes, active periods, and references from Test, Specimen, and Assay; define catalog authority and mapping concepts such as TestCatalogEntry, AssayCatalogEntry, and TestAssayMapping with source system, owner, version, status, effective_from/effective_to, and deprecation rules; and define TATMetricDefinition with formula authority, version, validity period, and consuming systems. This should close the authority and history gap before downstream EMR/LIS integration rules depend on unstable local representations.

  - source lenses: evolution
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - materiality: 이 문제는 결과/보고 상태가 EMR/LIS 간에 일관되게 진화하고 임상의에게 신뢰 가능한 상태를 제공해야 한다는 목적을 약화합니다. 상태 의미가 단일 권위가 아니라 코드명 매핑과 배치 동기화에 의존하므로, 확장 시 보고서의 권위 상태와 LIS 기록 상태가 분기할 수 있습니다.
  - root cause: 출발점은 Result와 Report가 하나의 canonical status authority 없이 각각 다른 enum vocabulary로 상태를 저장한다는 점입니다. Result.status의 preliminary/final/corrected와 Report.result_status의 prelim/finalized/amended가 같은 상태 흐름을 표현하지만 권위와 projection boundary가 분리되어 있지 않아 변경 책임이 두 곳으로 나뉩니다.
  - causal path: causal path는 Result.status가 별도 enum으로 정의되고, Report.result_status도 다른 이름의 별도 enum으로 정의된 뒤, Report.result_status가 임상의가 신뢰하는 권위 값처럼 주석 처리되는 흐름입니다. 여기에 corrected 변경 시 amended로 야간 배치 동기화해야 한다는 규칙이 붙어 두 상태가 독립적으로 움직일 수 있음이 드러납니다. 따라서 새 상태나 외부 표준 변경이 들어오면 enum 두 개와 동기화 규칙을 모두 맞춰야 하며, 누락이나 지연이 보고 신뢰를 손상시킬 수 있습니다.
  - action: ResultStatus를 canonical 상태 개념 또는 코드셋으로 분리하고, Report 상태는 그 canonical status의 명시적 projection이거나 별도 transition table로 정의해야 합니다. Report에 표시명이 필요하면 display mapping으로 두되, 상태 권위, projection 규칙, 동기화 지연 정책을 상태 모델 안에 포함해 다음 통합 설계가 중복 enum에 의존하지 않도록 먼저 닫아야 합니다.

- issue-010 (medium): Result.status와 Report.result_status는 같은 결과 상태를 가리키는 듯하지만 enum 이름, 권위 설명, 동기화 규칙이 서로 어긋나 있어 EMR/LIS 통합 설계에서 하나의 신뢰 가능한 상태 의미로 해석하기 어렵다.
  - issue stance agreement: 6/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: axiology, coverage, evolution, logic, semantics, structure
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6

  - remaining disagreement lenses: none
  - raised by lenses: semantics
  - issue statement: Result.status and Report.result_status have overlapping meanings, different enum names, and a delayed synchronization rule despite Report.result_status being described as clinician-trusted. Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml: Result.status and Report.result_status Source finding context: .onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:69, .onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:80, .onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:123 Source finding context: 동일한 결과 상태 의미가 서로 다른 enum 어휘와 권위 주장으로 모델링되어 있다. Source finding context: 프롬프트의 대상 목적은 EMR/LIS 연동 설계의 개념 권위 문서인데, 같은 결과 상태를 두 이름 체계와 두 권위 설명으로 두면 preliminary와 prelim, final과 finalized, corrected와 amended가 완전 동의어인지, 업무 단계 차이를 담은 별도 상태인지 해석이 갈린다. 특히 야간 1회 동기화 규칙은 Report.result_status가 임상의 신뢰값이라고 선언된 상태에서 지연된 파생값이 될 수 있음을 보여 주므로, 의미 권위가 흐려진다. Source finding context: 결과 상태의 canonical enum을 하나로 정하고 Report에는 그 상태의 표시/투영 필드임을 명시하거나, Report 상태가 별도 개념이라면 Result.status와 Report.result_status 간 전이 표를 명시해 의미 차이를 분리한다. 권위 값은 LIS Result.status인지 Report의 릴리스 상태인지 하나로 정리한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-001 Source finding context: EMR/LIS 통합의 개념 권위 문서로 결과 상태와 보고 상태의 의미를 일관되게 해석하게 하는 목적. Source finding context: EMR 또는 LIS가 result_status를 독립 권위 값으로 구현하거나 enum 이름을 단순 문자열로 매핑할 때 corrected/amended, final/finalized의 의미가 어긋난다. Source finding context: 임상의가 신뢰하는 보고 상태와 LIS가 기록한 결과 상태가 같은지 다른지 불명확해져 상태 동기화, 정정 보고, 완료 판단의 설계 신뢰도가 낮아진다. Source finding context: 동일한 결과 상태 개념을 Result와 Report에 중복 배치하면서 canonical 상태 어휘와 authority boundary를 정의하지 않았다. Source finding context: Result.status와 Report.result_status가 동일 정보라고 설명되지만 enum 값은 서로 다르다. Source finding context: 상태 규칙은 Result.status corrected가 Report.result_status amended로 바뀌어야 한다고 하여 두 필드 사이의 의미 매핑을 전제로 한다. Source finding context: Report.result_status는 임상의가 신뢰하는 권위 값이라고 선언되어 있어 파생/동기화 지연값인지 권위 원천인지 의미가 충돌한다.
  - affected purpose: EMR/LIS 통합의 개념 권위 문서로 결과 상태와 보고 상태의 의미를 일관되게 해석하게 하는 목적.
  - failure condition: EMR 또는 LIS가 result_status를 독립 권위 값으로 구현하거나 enum 이름을 단순 문자열로 매핑할 때 corrected/amended, final/finalized의 의미가 어긋난다.
  - impact: 임상의가 신뢰하는 보고 상태와 LIS가 기록한 결과 상태가 같은지 다른지 불명확해져 상태 동기화, 정정 보고, 완료 판단의 설계 신뢰도가 낮아진다.
  - root hypothesis: The ontology duplicates the result-state concept across Result and Report while leaving the authority boundary between canonical state and clinician-facing projection unresolved.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-001`, `materialized-input.md:69-72`, `materialized-input.md:80-83`, `materialized-input.md:121-123`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:69, .onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:80, .onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:123`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-012`, `finding-012.cause-001`, `finding-012.cause-002`, `materialized-input.md:123`, `finding-012.cause-003`, `materialized-input.md:83`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-010.axiology`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-010.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-010.evolution`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-003`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-010.logic`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/issue-ledger.yaml#issue-010`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/finding-relation-graph.yaml#rel-006`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-010.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-010.structure`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-003`, `rel-006`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: semantics
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - materiality: 이 문서의 목적은 EMR/LIS 통합에서 결과 상태와 보고 상태를 일관되게 해석하게 하는 개념 권위 문서가 되는 것이다. 그런데 final/finalized, corrected/amended 같은 값이 동의어인지 별도 업무 단계인지 불명확하면 EMR이나 LIS가 Report.result_status를 독립 권위 값으로 구현하거나 단순 문자열 매핑으로 처리할 수 있고, 그 결과 정정 보고, 완료 판단, 상태 동기화의 신뢰도가 낮아진다.
  - root cause: 문제의 시작점은 동일한 결과 상태 개념을 Result와 Report에 중복 배치하면서 canonical 상태 권위와 clinician-facing projection의 경계를 정하지 않은 것이다. 이 경계가 먼저 정해져야 Report.result_status가 LIS Result.status의 표시/투영인지, 아니면 별도 릴리스 상태인지 판단할 수 있다.
  - causal path: 먼저 Result.status와 Report.result_status가 동일 정보처럼 설명되지만 서로 다른 enum 어휘를 사용한다. 이어 상태 규칙은 Result.status corrected가 Report.result_status amended로 바뀌어야 한다고 하여 두 필드 사이의 의미 매핑을 전제한다. 마지막으로 Report.result_status는 임상의가 신뢰하는 권위 값이라고 선언되어 있어, 지연 동기화되는 파생값인지 독립 권위 원천인지 충돌한다. 이 경로가 중복 상태 모델링과 권위 경계 부재를 실제 해석 문제로 만든다.
  - action: 다음 단계 전에는 결과 상태의 canonical authority를 하나로 정해야 한다. 기본 해결은 LIS Result.status를 canonical enum으로 두고 Report.result_status를 표시/투영 필드로 명시하는 것이다. 만약 Report 상태가 별도 개념이라면 Result.status와 Report.result_status의 전이/투영 표, 지연 동기화 처리, corrected-to-amended 같은 매핑, 권위 우선순위를 명시해야 한다.

- issue-011 (medium): CriticalValue는 재사용 가능한 위험값 기준과 환자/결과별 통보 완료 상태를 한 개념에 섞고 있으므로, 위험 기준 개념과 실제 critical-result 통보 사건 개념으로 분리해야 한다.
  - issue stance agreement: 6/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: axiology, coverage, evolution, logic, semantics, structure
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6

  - remaining disagreement: 0/1 deliberation participants

  - issue statement: CriticalValue blends a critical threshold definition with a notified state that belongs to a concrete critical-result or notification event. Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml: CriticalValue Source finding context: .onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:100, .onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:106 Source finding context: CriticalValue가 기준 범위와 특정 통보 사건 상태를 한 개념에 섞고 있다. Source finding context: 위험 결과 값 범위는 검사별 판정 기준 또는 rule에 가깝고, notified는 특정 환자/결과 인스턴스에서 발생하는 통보 이행 상태다. 기준과 사건을 같은 엔티티에 넣으면 어떤 CriticalValue 인스턴스가 '위험 기준'인지 '위험 결과 발생 건'인지 의미가 불분명해진다. Source finding context: CriticalValueRule 또는 CriticalThreshold를 기준 개념으로 두고, 특정 Result가 기준에 걸린 사건은 CriticalResultAlert/CriticalNotification 같은 별도 개념으로 분리한다. notified, notified_at, recipient는 사건/통보 개념에 둔다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-003 Source finding context: 임상검사 워크플로에서 위험 결과 통보의 개념 권위와 운영 상태를 명확히 모델링하는 목적. Source finding context: 시스템이 CriticalValue를 기준 테이블로도, 환자별 통보 상태 테이블로도 해석해야 하는 경우 기준 변경과 통보 이력 기록의 의미가 충돌한다. Source finding context: 위험값 알림 누락, 기준값 변경 시 이력 오염, 통보 완료 여부의 소속 불명확성이 생겨 운영 위험을 만든다. Source finding context: 판정 기준 개념과 통보 이행 사건 개념을 분리하지 않았다. Source finding context: CriticalValue 정의와 lower_bound/upper_bound는 검사별 위험 범위 기준을 나타낸다. Source finding context: notified는 특정 통보 완료 상태이고 통보 시각/수신자라는 사건 속성과 연결된다. Source finding context: 기준 속성과 사건 상태가 같은 엔티티에 공존해 인스턴스의 존재론적 유형이 불명확해진다.
  - affected purpose: 임상검사 워크플로에서 위험 결과 통보의 개념 권위와 운영 상태를 명확히 모델링하는 목적.
  - failure condition: 시스템이 CriticalValue를 기준 테이블로도, 환자별 통보 상태 테이블로도 해석해야 하는 경우 기준 변경과 통보 이력 기록의 의미가 충돌한다.
  - impact: 위험값 알림 누락, 기준값 변경 시 이력 오염, 통보 완료 여부의 소속 불명확성이 생겨 운영 위험을 만든다.
  - root hypothesis: CriticalValue combines threshold-rule meaning and patient/result-specific notification-event state in one concept.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-003`, `materialized-input.md:100-106`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:100, .onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:106`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-014`, `finding-014.cause-001`, `materialized-input.md:100-105`, `finding-014.cause-002`, `materialized-input.md:106`, `finding-014.cause-003`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-011.axiology`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-011.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-011.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-011.logic`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/issue-ledger.yaml#issue-011`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/finding-relation-graph.yaml#rel-002`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-011.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-011.structure`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-001`, `rel-002`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: semantics
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - materiality: 이 문제는 임상검사 워크플로에서 위험 결과 통보의 개념 권위와 운영 상태를 명확히 모델링하려는 목적을 약화한다. 같은 CriticalValue 인스턴스가 기준 테이블인지 환자별 통보 상태인지 불분명하면 기준 변경, 통보 이력, 통보 완료 여부의 소속이 충돌하여 위험값 알림 누락이나 이력 오염으로 이어질 수 있다.
  - root cause: 출발점은 CriticalValue가 threshold-rule 의미와 patient/result-specific notification-event 상태를 함께 가진다는 점이다. lower_bound와 upper_bound는 검사별 위험 범위 기준에 속하지만, notified는 특정 결과가 발생한 뒤 통보가 이행되었는지를 나타내는 사건 상태이므로 identity와 lifecycle이 다르다.
  - causal path: CriticalValue 정의와 범위 속성은 먼저 재사용 가능한 위험 판정 기준을 만든다. 여기에 notified 같은 통보 완료 상태가 붙으면서 동일 개념이 기준 권위와 사건 이행 증거를 동시에 대표하게 되고, 그 결과 인스턴스가 '위험 기준'인지 '위험 결과 발생 및 통보 건'인지 존재론적으로 불명확해진다.
  - action: CriticalValue를 CriticalValueRule 또는 CriticalThreshold 같은 기준 개념과 CriticalResultAlert 또는 CriticalNotification 같은 사건/통보 개념으로 분리해야 한다. notified, notified_at, recipient 및 통보 증거는 사건/통보 쪽에 두고, 기준 개념은 검사별 판정 범위와 rule 권위만 맡게 하는 것이 다음 단계의 critical-value workflow 관계를 만들기 전 필요한 정리다.

- issue-012 (medium): STAT 주문 의미가 Order.priority 값, Order.is_stat boolean, StatOrder subtype에 동시에 들어 있어 canonical 기준이 불명확합니다. 이 상태에서는 STAT 라우팅, SLA 적용, stat_reason 검증이 서로 다른 표현을 기준으로 갈라질 수 있으므로 다음 모델링 단계 전에 정리해야 하는 material issue입니다.

  - issue statement: STAT order semantics are duplicated across priority, is_stat, and StatOrder, making routing and validation rules ambiguous. Source finding context: clinical-lab-ontology.yaml: STAT order modeling Source finding context: .onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:21, .onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:22, .onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:25 Source finding context: STAT 의미가 priority 값, boolean, subtype으로 동시에 표현되어 있다. Source finding context: STAT는 주문의 우선순위 상태인지, 별도 주문 유형인지, boolean 플래그인지 의미가 고정되어야 한다. 세 표현이 모두 있으면 priority=urgent와 is_stat=true의 차이, priority=stat와 StatOrder 인스턴스의 관계, stat_reason의 필수 조건이 불명확하다. Source finding context: STAT를 priority enum의 한 값으로 유지할지, 별도 subtype으로 둘지 하나를 canonical 표현으로 정한다. subtype을 유지한다면 is_stat는 파생값으로 표시하고 priority=stat와 StatOrder의 불변조건 및 stat_reason 요구 조건을 명시한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-004 Source finding context: 주문 상태와 우선순위 의미를 EMR/LIS 통합에서 일관되게 전달하는 목적. Source finding context: 인터페이스나 업무 규칙이 priority, is_stat, StatOrder 중 서로 다른 필드를 기준으로 STAT 라우팅, SLA, 사유 필수 여부를 판단할 때 실패한다. Source finding context: 응급 주문 처리 의미가 분산되어 STAT 누락, 중복 라우팅, 사유 검증 누락 같은 운영 위험이 생긴다. Source finding context: STAT라는 하나의 업무 의미를 세 가지 모델링 방식으로 중복 표현했다. Source finding context: Order.priority enum에 stat가 포함된다. Source finding context: Order.is_stat boolean도 동일한 STAT 여부를 기록한다고 설명한다. Source finding context: StatOrder subtype까지 추가되어 STAT의 canonical 의미 위치가 불명확해진다.
  - affected purpose: 주문 상태와 우선순위 의미를 EMR/LIS 통합에서 일관되게 전달하는 목적.
  - failure condition: 인터페이스나 업무 규칙이 priority, is_stat, StatOrder 중 서로 다른 필드를 기준으로 STAT 라우팅, SLA, 사유 필수 여부를 판단할 때 실패한다.
  - impact: 응급 주문 처리 의미가 분산되어 STAT 누락, 중복 라우팅, 사유 검증 누락 같은 운영 위험이 생긴다.
  - root hypothesis: STAT is represented simultaneously as an Order priority value, a boolean flag, and a subtype, without a canonical meaning location or invariants.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-004`, `materialized-input.md:21-29`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:21, .onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:22, .onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/execution-preparation/materialized-input.md:25`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-015`, `finding-015.cause-001`, `materialized-input.md:21`, `finding-015.cause-002`, `materialized-input.md:22`, `finding-015.cause-003`, `materialized-input.md:25-29`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-012.axiology`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-012.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-012.evolution`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-012.logic`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/issue-ledger.yaml#issue-012`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-012.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-012.structure`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260611-435e4f4c/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: semantics
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - materiality: 이 이슈는 주문 상태와 우선순위 의미를 EMR/LIS 통합에서 일관되게 전달하려는 목적을 약화시킵니다. 인터페이스나 업무 규칙이 priority, is_stat, StatOrder 중 다른 필드를 기준으로 STAT 여부를 판단하면 응급 주문이 누락되거나 중복 라우팅되고, STAT 사유 필수 검증도 빠질 수 있습니다.
  - root cause: 출발점은 STAT라는 하나의 업무 의미가 세 가지 모델링 방식으로 중복 표현된 것입니다. priority enum의 stat, is_stat boolean, StatOrder subtype 중 어느 것이 원천 의미인지 정해지지 않았기 때문에 나머지를 파생값이나 제약으로 묶는 불변조건도 세울 수 없습니다.
  - causal path: causal path는 Order.priority에 stat가 포함되고, 이어 Order.is_stat도 동일한 STAT 여부를 기록하며, 마지막으로 StatOrder subtype까지 추가되어 canonical 의미 위치가 흐려진다는 흐름입니다. 이 조합은 priority=urgent와 is_stat=true의 차이, priority=stat와 StatOrder 인스턴스의 관계, stat_reason 필수 조건을 모두 애매하게 만듭니다.
  - action: STAT 표현 하나를 canonical로 선택해야 합니다. priority enum 값을 유지할지, StatOrder subtype을 canonical로 둘지 먼저 정하고, 남는 필드는 파생 projection으로 정의한 뒤 priority=stat, is_stat=true, StatOrder, stat_reason 요구 조건 사이의 불변조건을 명시해야 합니다. 이 결정이 먼저 정리되어야 라우팅, SLA, 검증 규칙이 같은 기준을 사용할 수 있습니다.

- issue-014 (medium): Issue-014는 CriticalValue가 실제 Result, Report, 통보/확인 흐름과 구조적으로 연결되지 않은 문제입니다. 위험값 판정 규칙은 존재하지만, 어떤 실제 검사 결과가 위험값을 발생시키고 그 결과가 보고 또는 책임 있는 통보 경로로 이어지는지가 온톨로지 관계 그래프 안에서 닫히지 않습니다.
